PRECEDENCE RULE

  1. The same key may appear on several records. The record with the latest as_of date wins.
  2. If the dates are equal, source=AUDIT beats source=SELF-REPORT.
  3. If still tied, the higher rev= number wins.

Position in the file is irrelevant — a superseded record can appear anywhere, including after the record that supersedes it. Identifiers are deliberately similar to one another; a near-match is a wrong match.
